Method: 使用三种不同算法从相同源图像生成不同复杂度的纹理，采用基于秩的统计量量化CNN和三种ViT内部表征中的信息编码，并将这些表征与人类心理物理学数据进行比较。

Result: 不同ViT之间的纹理表征对齐，但ViT与CNN之间不对齐；ViT对不同复杂度的纹理形成相似表征；ViT的表征比CNN更能预测人类识别纹理的表现。

Conclusion: ViT可能比CNN更忠实地捕捉人类对纹理模式的视觉处理方式，且计算模型中的纹理刺激表征可能受网络架构驱动。

Abstract: In computational vision science, Convolutional Neural Networks (CNNs) have emerged as a popular model of biological vision because of the alignment they can exhibit with neural and behavioral data in humans and animals. However, it remains unclear to what extent this alignment persists for visual tasks that extend beyond the canonical object recognition paradigm based on well defined semantic content. In this study, we diverge from the common object-centric view by focusing on another aspect of vision: texture perception. We consider textures of different complexity generated with three different algorithms from the same source images. Using a rank-based statistic, we quantify the information encoded in the internal representations of a CNN and three Vision Transformers (ViTs), and we compare the similarity of these representations to those inferred from human psychophysics data. We find that the representation of textures is aligned in different ViTs, but not between the ViTs and the CNN; that ViTs form similar representations for textures of different complexity; that human performance in recognizing textures can be better predicted from ViTs representations rather than CNN representations. Taken together, these results suggest that ViTs may capture more faithfully than CNNs how texture patterns are visually processed by humans, and that the representations of texture stimuli in computational models may be driven by the network architecture.

</details>


### [99] [WCog-VLA: A Dual-Level World-Cognitive Vision-Language-Action Model for End-to-End Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08375)
*Xuerun Yan,Zhexi Lian,Nuoheng Zhang,Shiyu Fang,Haoran Wang,Chen Lv,Jia Hu,Binyang Song*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: WCog-VLA 提出了一种双层级世界认知视觉-语言-动作框架，通过语义级世界预测（3D感知+Game-CoT推理）和生成级世界演化（ADDT生成多智能体轨迹）实现主动自动驾驶，在NAVSIM基准上达到92.9 PDMS的SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA模型要么缺乏全面的世界认知，要么存在碎片化的世界预测，本质上限制了它们只能进行反应式驾驶，无法实现主动的、具有前瞻性的自动驾驶。

Method: 提出双层级WCog-VLA框架：1) 语义层面，通过3D空间感知和智能体token注入统一世界认知与推理，并实现博弈论链式思考（Game-CoT）推理；2) 生成层面，提出对齐解耦扩散Transformer（ADDT）作为生成式世界模型，合成物理合理的多智能体联合轨迹，并通过场景表示对齐减少去噪步数以加速推理。

Result: 在NAVSIM基准上取得92.9 PDMS分数，达到当前最优（SOTA）性能。同时构建了包含85k Game-CoT标注的大规模数据集。

Conclusion: WCog-VLA成功连接了语义世界预测与生成世界演化，弥补了现有VLA模型在主动驾驶能力上的不足，为实现更安全、更具前瞻性的自动驾驶系统提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models have advanced end-to-end autonomous driving. However, existing methods either lack comprehensive world cognition or suffer from fragmented world foresight, inherently confining these models to reactive driving. To address this limitation, we propose WCog-VLA, a novel dual-level World-Cognitive VLA framework that successfully bridges semantic world forecasting with generative world evolution to achieve proactive autonomous driving. At the semantic level, WCog-VLA unifies world cognition and reasoning by incorporating 3D spatial perception and injecting agent tokens to capture the world dynamics, while concurrently enabling Game-theoretic Chain-of-Thought (Game-CoT) reasoning. At the generative level, we introduce the Aligned Decoupled Diffusion Transformer (ADDT) as a powerful generative world model that synthesizes physically-plausible joint multi-agent trajectories. Through scene representation alignment, ADDT reduces the number of denoising steps required and thus significantly accelerates inference. To facilitate strategic reasoning, we further construct a large-scale dataset featuring 85k Game-CoT annotations. Extensive experiments on the NAVSIM benchmark demonstrate that WCog-VLA achieves a State-Of-The-Art (SOTA) PDMS score of 92.9.

</details>


### [100] [Classical versus Deep Mirror-Symmetry Scoring: A Benchmark of Thirteen Methods](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08379)
*Maximilian Woehrer*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文对13种图像对称性评分方法（含9种已有方法和4种新方法）进行了系统性基准测试，采用反射精确协议和统计学显著性检验。结果表明，在单轴数据集上深度特征表现最佳，但在多轴协议中，经典HOG描述符与最佳深度特征差距很小（虽显著），且速度快约300倍。歧视性信息集中于中等尺度的定向特征。本文发布了开源工具包imgsym。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图像对称性评分方法众多，但缺乏在统一、统计严谨的协议下进行比较，无法客观评估各方法的优劣。

Method: 收集9种已有方法并引入4种新方法，共计13种评分方法；采用反射精确协议，以机会水平为锚点进行显著性检验的判别能力评估；在4个单轴和5个多轴数据集上进行基准测试。

Result: 深度骨干网络在单轴和困难多轴协议上表现最佳；但HOG描述符与最佳冻结网络特征差距很小（虽显著），且与排名第二的CNN滤波器方法无统计可区分性，运行速度快约300倍；歧视性信息集中于中等尺度的定向特征。

Conclusion: 对于对称性测量，冻结深度特征相比调优的经典描述符优势有限；任务训练的深度评分器是否能取得更好效果仍有待探索；发布了开源工具包imgsym。

Abstract: Quantifying how mirror-symmetric an image is about a given axis (symmetry scoring) underpins applications from visual aesthetics to medical imaging, yet proposed scoring methods have never been compared on a common, statistically grounded protocol. We benchmark 13 scoring methods (nine collected from literature, four introduced here) spanning from classical features to frozen deep features, across four single-axis and five multi-axis datasets under a reflection-exact protocol with a chance-anchored, significance-tested discrimination skill. Deep backbones perform best on single-axis and harder multi-axis protocols. However, a classical histogram-of-oriented-gradients (HOG) descriptor trails the best frozen-network readout by a small (but significant) margin, is not statistically separable from the runner-up (a CNN-filter measure), and runs ~300x faster on CPU. Our results show that discrimination concentrates in mid-scale oriented features, where deep backbones peak at a low or mid stage, and HOG peaks at a mid cell size. Among existing methods, frozen deep features thus offer little over a tuned classical descriptor for measuring symmetry; whether task-trained deep scorers can do better remains open. We release the scorers and harness in imgsym, an open toolkit for image symmetry detection and measurement.

</details>


### [101] [Attribute Retrieving for Open-Vocabulary Endoscopic Compositional Referring Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08397)
*Shun Liu,Nan Xi,Yang Liu,Tianyu Luan,Xuan Gong,David Doermann*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文针对内窥镜图像中参照分割（RIS）任务，提出了大规模基准数据集ReferEndoscopy和基于属性检索的AR-ERIS框架，实现了开放词汇的合成参照分割，在模拟和真实内窥镜数据上均达到最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将RIS扩展到内窥镜图像面临标注稀缺、领域特异性图文关系复杂等挑战，现有视觉语言模型无法捕捉细粒度文本线索，导致性能不佳且泛化有限。

Method: 构建了大规模内窥镜RIS基准数据集ReferEndoscopy，并提出AR-ERIS框架，利用属性检索实现开放词汇的内窥镜合成参照分割，并在该数据集上预训练。

Result: 在模拟和真实内窥镜数据上均达到最先进性能，并表现出强泛化能力。

Conclusion: AR-ERIS框架有效解决了内窥镜场景下RIS的标注稀缺和细粒度理解难题，数据集和代码将开源。

Abstract: Referring Image Segmentation (RIS) aims to segment image regions specified by natural language, enabling fine-grained and controllable visual understanding. Extending RIS to endoscopic imagery, however, presents unique challenges, including scarce high-quality annotations and complex, domain-specific image-text relationships. Although recent vision-language models demonstrate strong cross-domain alignment, they often fail to capture fine-grained textual cues in endoscopic settings, resulting in suboptimal performance and limited generalization. To address these challenges, we introduce ReferEndoscopy, a large-scale benchmark for RIS in the endoscopy field. Building on this dataset, we propose the Attribute Retrieval-based Endoscopic-RIS (AR-ERIS) framework for open-vocabulary endoscopic compositional referring segmentation. AR-ERIS leverages attribute retrieval for open-vocabulary endoscopic compositional referring segmentation and is pretrained on the curated ReferEndoscopy dataset, achieving state-of-the-art performance with strong generalization across both simulated and real-world endoscopic data. The dataset and code will be publicly released upon completion of the review process.

</details>


### [102] [Swapping Faces, Saving Features: A Dual-Purpose Pipeline for Pedestrian Privacy in ITS](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08402)
*Roba H. Farouk,Catherine M. Elias*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种五阶段流水线，通过面部交换技术在保护行人隐私（身份隐藏）的同时保留必要面部属性，以平衡自动驾驶数据集Egy-DRiVeS中的隐私与数据可用性，并实验证明Roop模型优于Ghost-v2。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动驾驶车辆训练需要大量行人图像数据，但直接使用这些数据集存在身份盗窃、行人跟踪等严重安全风险。现有隐私保护方法虽能保护隐私，但会降低图像可用性，影响模型训练效果。因此需要一种既能保护隐私又不破坏数据可用性的方法。

Method: 实现了一个五阶段流水线，通过面部交换技术保护行人隐私。具体评估了Roop和Ghost-v2两种面部交换模型在Egy-DRiVeS数据集上的表现，选择性能更优的Roop模型来平衡隐私保护（身份隐藏）与数据可用性（面部属性保留）。

Result: Roop面部交换模型在多个方面优于Ghost-v2模型，被选为五阶段流水线的核心模型，能够有效在身份隐藏（隐私）和面部属性保留（可用性）之间取得平衡。

Conclusion: 通过Roop面部交换模型实现的五阶段流水线，能够在保护行人隐私的同时保持关键面部属性，满足Egy-DRiVeS数据集的隐私需求，为自动驾驶智能交通系统中的行人轨迹预测模型提供可用且隐私安全的数据。

Abstract: Large-scale and diverse datasets are needed to train AI models to take real-time decisions for autonomous vehicles (AVs), an intelligent transportation system (ITS) application. Pedestrian intention and trajectory prediction are critical models used in AVs, requiring datasets involving diverse pedestrian images. Unrestricted access to these datasets imposes serious security risks, like identity theft and pedestrian tracking. The challenge is to apply privacy preservation procedures while maintaining the image attributes needed to train the models. Existing privacy methods may preserve the pedestrian's privacy, but degrade the image usability, which hinders the models' effectiveness. This work's focus is to implement a five-stage pipeline to protect pedestrians' privacy through face swapping while keeping the essential facial attributes intact. It should be tailored to satisfy the privacy needs of the Egy-DRiVeS dataset. Moreover, Roop and Ghost-v2 face-swapping models are evaluated. Provenly, Roop outperforms Ghost-v2 in various aspects, as will be discussed. Consequently, Roop is the face-swapping model to be used in the pipeline to strike the balance between pedestrian privacy via identity concealment and data usability via facial attribute preservation.

</details>


### [103] [Track2Map: Online Deformable SLAM with Motion-Aware Pose Optimization in Robotic Surgery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08408)
*Tianyi Song,Sierra Bonilla,Xinwei Ju,Evangelos Mazomenos,Danail Stoyanov,Adam Schmidt,Omid Mohareri,Sophia Bano,Francisco Vasconcelos*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Track2Map是一种在线3D高斯溅射SLAM方法，能够在缺乏或存在噪声的相机轨迹先验下，直接从手术视频中联合优化相机轨迹与可变形三维场景表示，通过密集2D点轨迹锚定的变形初始化和静态相机周期检测来稳定优化，在StereoMIS数据集上相比竞争方法取得了更优的重建质量和相机轨迹估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于高斯溅射的密集可变形3D解剖结构重建方法大多为离线方式，且依赖准确的相机轨迹先验（如机器人运动学数据），当先验缺失或存在噪声时适用性受限。

Method: 提出Track2Map在线管道，联合优化相机轨迹和可变形3D场景表示；引入轨道锚定的变形初始化（利用密集2D点轨迹）；利用轨迹统计检测静态相机周期以减少增量建图中的漂移，从而解耦相机运动与场景变形。

Result: 在StereoMIS数据集上，Track2Map在重建质量和相机轨迹估计方面优于竞争的SLAM方法，以及使用相机轨迹先验的非SLAM方法。

Conclusion: Track2Map作为在线SLAM方法，能够在缺乏或存在噪声的相机先验下实现鲁棒的3D重建，有效扩展了高斯溅落在机器人辅助微创手术中的实际应用场景。

Abstract: Gaussian splatting is the current state-of-the-art for dense, deformable 3D anatomy reconstruction in robot-assisted minimally invasive surgery (RAMIS); however, most pipelines are offline and depend on accurate camera trajectory priors (often from robotic kinematics), limiting applicability when priors are missing or noisy. To address these limitations, we propose Track2Map, an online 3D Gaussian Splatting pipeline that jointly optimizes camera trajectory and 3D deformable scene representation directly from surgical video. Track2Map is therefore capable of robust 3D reconstructions when camera trajectory priors are either absent or noisy, and due to its online nature it effectively works as a Simultaneous Localisation and Mapping (SLAM) method. To stabilize optimization in the presence of tissue motion and ambiguous visual cues, we introduce a track-anchored deformation initialization using dense 2D point tracks. Track statistics are further utilized to disentangle camera motion from scene deformation by detecting static camera periods and reducing drift during incremental mapping. Experiments on StereoMIS show improved reconstruction quality and camera trajectory against competing SLAM methods, as well as compared to non-SLAM methods that utilize camera trajectory priors. The code is available at https://track2map.github.io/.

</details>


### [104] [DeltaV: Thinking with Visual State Updates in Unified Large Multimodal Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08434)
*Pengjie Wang,Linger Deng,Zujia Zhang,Shaojie Zhang,Zhenbo Luo,Pei Fu,Jian Luan,Xiang Bai,Yuliang Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出DeltaV，一种用视觉增量更新替代全图生成的多模态大模型，通过紧凑的更新token捕获视觉变化，并引入TSIM路由器和StructCoT数据集，显著减少视觉token冗余并提升推理性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有统一多模态大模型在交错推理中采用全图生成范式，导致大量视觉token冗余，且对推理关键的状态变化监督不足。

Method: 提出DeltaV模型：1) 基于历史视觉状态预测紧凑的增量更新token，避免重复建模不变内容；2) 引入TSIM路由器，当边际重构增益低于阈值时停止分配token，使token预算与视觉变化幅度对齐；3) 构建包含105万样本、覆盖44个任务领域的StructCoT数据集。

Result: 新生成的视觉token平均减少55.6%，且不损失重构保真度；多模态推理提升3.3%；DeltaV-2B在领域内评测上超越更大规模开源模型8.4%，在外部评测上超越同规模的Qwen3-VL-2B达5.9%。

Conclusion: 视觉增量更新范式有效替代全图生成，在降低计算冗余的同时提升多模态推理性能，结合大规模结构化数据训练可达到超越更大模型的效果。

Abstract: Current Unified Large Multimodal Models (ULMMs) support interleaved multimodal reasoning through textual reasoning and intermediate visual states, but typically generate each visual state as a full image. This full-image generation paradigm introduces substantial visual-token redundancy and dilutes supervision on sparse yet reasoning-critical state transitions. We propose DeltaV, a ULMM that replaces full-image generation with visual updates. Conditioned on historical visual states, DeltaV incrementally predicts compact update tokens that capture the visual changes across reasoning steps, avoiding repeated modeling of unchanged content. To align the token budget of each update with the magnitude of visual change, DeltaV introduces a temporal similarity (TSIM) Router, which stops allocating tokens once the marginal reconstruction gain falls below a threshold. To support more diverse and generalizable reasoning, we further construct StructCoT, a large-scale interleaved multimodal reasoning dataset with 1.05M samples spanning 44 task domains. Experiments show that the visual-update paradigm reduces newly generated visual tokens by 55.6\% on average without compromising reconstruction fidelity, and improves multimodal reasoning by 3.3\% over full-image generation. Trained with StructCoT and large-scale multimodal data, DeltaV-2B further outperforms substantially larger open-source models by 8.4\% on in-domain multimodal reasoning evaluations and surpasses the comparable-scale Qwen3-VL-2B by 5.9\% on external multimodal reasoning and understanding benchmarks. Code, models, and StructCoT will be released at https://github.com/Pengjie-W/DeltaV.

</details>


### [105] [Predicting Viticulture Potential through an Ensemble of U-Net and a Geospatial Foundation Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08449)
*Jorge Ignacio Perez,Hwaai Kang Kee,Lucas Rassbach*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文介绍了DS@GT ARC团队在ImageCLEF AI4Agri 2026 Subtask 1中的方法，通过集成U-Net和地理空间基础模型Prithvi-2.0预测法国南部葡萄栽培潜力，最终在排行榜上以68.32的±1准确率位列7支队伍中的第2名。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统农业潜力评估方法（调查、实地测量、土壤测试等）成本高昂，亟需利用遥感数据等更经济高效的替代方案来支撑可持续土地管理和农业规划。

Method: 采用集成学习方法，将U-Net与地理空间基础模型Prithvi-2.0相结合，构建混合模型用于葡萄栽培潜力预测。

Result: 最佳模型在排行榜上取得68.32的±1准确率，在7支参赛队伍中排名第2。

Conclusion: 集成U-Net与地理空间基础模型的方法在葡萄栽培潜力预测任务上表现优异，代码已开源供社区进一步研究和应用。

Abstract: Determining agricultural potential is fundamental to sustainable land management and agricultural planning. Remote sensing data is increasingly valuable as an avenue for agricultural potential due to the cost of traditional methods (surveys, in-situ measurements, soil testing, etc). ImageCLEF AI4Agri 2026: Subtask 1 is concerned with the prediction of viticulture potential in Southern France. The DS@GT ARC's submission for Subtask 1 introduces an ensemble of U-Net and a Geospatial Foundation Model (Prithvi-2.0). Our best model achieved a $\pm$1 accuracy of 68.32 on the leaderboard, ranking 2nd among 7 teams. The implementation for this work is publicly available at https://github.com/dsgt-arc/imageclef-ai4agri-2026 .

</details>


### [106] [VEGAS: Human-Aligned Video Caption Evaluation via Gaze](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08489)
*Shenghui Chen,Po-han Li,Ximeng Sun,Shijia Yang,Emad Barsoum,Zicheng Liu,Sandeep Chinchali,Ufuk Topcu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出VEGAS，一种无需训练的指标，利用测试时的眼动数据（gaze）对视频描述进行重排序，使其与观看者的注意力对齐，从而生成个性化的视频字幕。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的视频描述生成方法虽然质量高，但无法捕捉不同观看者的个体注意力差异，生成的描述缺乏个性化。作者希望利用眼动信号（gaze）来引导字幕选择，使其与观看者的实际关注点一致。

Method: 提出VEGAS指标，基于信息论和跨模态对齐，量化候选描述与观看者眼动数据之间的匹配程度；通过拒绝采样（rejection sampling）从候选描述中选出最优结果，无需重新训练模型。

Result: 在自我中心活动和教学幻灯片等数据集上，VEGAS选择的描述与人类注意力对齐显著更好，并且提升了下游字幕到视频检索的性能。

Conclusion: 将观看者注意力（眼动数据）融入推理阶段可以显著提升视频描述的个性化程度和实际可用性，VEGAS是一种实用且无需训练的有效方法。

Abstract: Vision-language models excel at video captioning, yet typically generate descriptions that fail to capture individual viewers' attention. We propose VEGAS (Video caption Evaluation via GAze Score), a training-free metric that leverages test-time gaze to sample personalized, attention-aligned text. It is a cross-modal, information-theoretic metric that quantifies how well a candidate caption matches a viewer's focus. To evaluate VEGAS, we curate a dataset of egocentric activities and instructional slides paired with synchronized gaze and reference annotations. We then select captions based on VEGAS via rejection sampling without model retraining. Experiments show that VEGAS-selected captions align significantly better with human focus and improve downstream caption-to-video retrieval, demonstrating the practical utility of incorporating viewer attention during inference.

</details>


### [107] [Cognitive-structured Multimodal Agent for Multimodal Understanding, Generation, and Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08497)
*Feng Wang,Canmiao Fu,Zhipeng Huang,Chen Li,Jing Lyu,Ge Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种认知结构化多模态智能体（CMA），通过情景视觉记忆将视觉信息外化，在长程多模态对话中显著提升检索准确率（20轮会话中达91.4%）并降低推理时间（23.1s→12.7s），超越32B基线模型8.2%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有统一多模态模型将所有历史视觉和文本输入重复送入共享上下文窗口，导致视觉token爆炸和跨轮引用不可靠，限制了长程多模态对话能力。

Method: 提出认知结构化多模态智能体（CMA），包含三个核心模块：感知抽象引擎（Perceptual Abstraction Engine）进行结构化视觉抽象、认知检索引擎（Cognitive Retrieval Engine）进行跨轮记忆检索、多模态执行控制器（Multimodal Executive Controller）进行自主任务推理和动作规划。并构建统一场景引擎（Unified Scenario Engine）生成带细粒度检索标注的多轮对话以支持强化学习优化。

Result: 8B参数的CMA在20轮会话中达到91.4%的检索准确率，超越32B基线模型8.2%，同时将每轮推理时间从23.1秒降低至12.7秒。

Conclusion: 结构化记忆和模块化决策为长程多模态智能体提供了比单一参数扩展更可扩展、更高效的范式。

Abstract: Recent unified multimodal models show a single architecture can jointly perform vision/language understanding and image generation/editing. However, they repeatedly feed all historical visual and textual inputs into a shared context window, limiting long-horizon multimodal dialogue due to visual token explosion and unreliable cross-turn referencing. We propose a Cognitive-structured Multimodal Agent that externalizes visual information into an Episodic Visual Memory and selectively reactivates relevant episodes during reasoning. The agent consists of a Perceptual Abstraction Engine for structured visual abstraction, a Cognitive Retrieval Engine for cross-turn memory retrieval, and a Multimodal Executive Controller for autonomous task inference and action planning. To address the lack of turn-level retrieval supervision in existing datasets, we develop a Unified Scenario Engine that programmatically generates structured multi-turn conversations with fine-grained retrieval annotations, enabling reinforcement learning to optimize abstraction and retrieval policies. We also construct a long-horizon visual-dialogue benchmark stratified by difficulty to evaluate episodic visual recall. Our 8B agent achieves 91.4% retrieval accuracy over 20-turn sessions, surpassing 32B baselines by +8.2% while nearly halving per-turn inference time (23.1s -> 12.7s). We further present the Cognitive-structured Multimodal Agent Harness (CMA-Harness), a tool-augmented deployment of the same cognitive structure integrating persistent multimodal memory, web access, image generation/editing/composition tools, and OpenAI-compatible serving. Structured memory and modular decision-making offer a more scalable, efficient paradigm for long-horizon multimodal agents than monolithic parameter scaling. Code: https://github.com/caseclose/cma-harness ; Project page: https://caseclose.github.io/cma-harness/

</details>


### [108] [CT-CLIP Representations for Multimodal Lung Cancer Survival Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08503)
*Sofie Allgöwer,Mikael Johansson,Andreas Hallqvist,Jonas Andersson,Åse Johnsson,Ida Häggström,Jennifer Alvén*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本研究评估了领域特定的基础模型CT-CLIP在数据有限的临床场景中用于肺癌多模态生存预测的效果。冻结的CT-CLIP特征提取器配合轻量级生存预测头，在242名患者的数据上优于临床基线，并与其它多模态方法性能相当或更优，能有效区分高、低风险患者。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习驱动的生存模型常受限于缺乏高质量、带可靠预后结果的大规模影像队列，因此需要探索在数据受限的临床环境下如何利用领域特定的基础模型进行多模态生存预测。

Method: 使用CT-CLIP基础模型作为特征提取器，结合冻结编码器、全微调、低秩适配等适配策略，融合预处理CT影像和临床变量（242名肺癌患者），与临床和多模态基线进行对比及模态消融实验。

Result: 冻结的CT-CLIP模型配合可训练的轻量级生存预测头，超越了临床基线，性能与其他多模态方法相当或更优，并能将患者区分为临床有意义的高风险与低风险组。

Conclusion: 在数据受限的临床环境中，利用领域特定的基础模型（如CT-CLIP）作为冻结特征提取器，可有效进行多模态生存预测，为肺癌治疗规划提供可靠的预后工具。

Abstract: Accurate prognosis prediction is important for treatment planning in lung cancer, but deep learning-driven survival modelling is often limited by the scarcity of curated imaging cohorts with reliable outcome data. This study evaluates whether representations from a domain-specific foundation model can be used for multimodal survival prediction in data-constrained clinical settings. We assess the foundation model CT-CLIP as a feature extractor for pretreatment computed tomography images and clinical variables from 242 diagnosed lung cancer patients. The evaluation includes adaptation strategies based on frozen encoders, full fine-tuning, and low-rank adaptation, together with modality ablations and comparisons with clinical and multimodal baselines. The results show that a frozen CT-CLIP model combined with a trainable lightweight survival head outperforms the clinical baseline and achieves comparable or improved performance relative to other multimodal approaches, and separates patients into clinically meaningful high- and low-risk groups.

</details>


### [109] [Do Egocentric Video-Language Models Capture Both Hand- and Object-Centric Cues?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08514)
*Masatoshi Tateno,Alexandros Stergiou,Risa Shinoda,Yoichi Sato,Dima Damen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结合手-对象掩码训练和HOI动态感知解码器的新学习范式，以解决手-对象交互识别中模型依赖虚假相关性的问题。同时引入CI-HOI评估基准和DEHOI测试集，证明该方法在多种任务上优于现有模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频-语言模型在手-对象交互识别中存在捷径学习问题，即依赖手、对象或环境上下文之间的虚假相关性进行预测，而非真正基于手和对象本身的表观与动态进行推理，导致模型鲁棒性不足。

Method: 提出一种新学习范式：(i) 手-对象掩码训练——从部分手或对象观测中进行鲁棒推理；(ii) HOI动态感知解码器——通过辅助预测手和对象的位置及语义，显式学习以手和对象为中心的嵌入表示，增强对两类线索的敏感性。同时构建CI-HOI评估框架和DEHOI测试集（通过修补技术分离手和对象观测），实现解耦化的HOI评估。

Result: 在DEHOI测试集、标准动作识别、物体状态识别以及机器人操作动作识别任务上，该方法均优于现有模型，更有效地利用手和对象中心信息，实现更鲁棒的HOI理解。

Conclusion: 所提出的训练策略能够比现有模型更有效地利用手和对象中心信息，提升HOI理解的鲁棒性，并在多个下游任务中取得一致改进。

Abstract: Hand-object interaction (HOI) recognition requires capturing both hand manipulations and object transformations. However, existing video-language models often fall into shortcuts by relying on spurious correlations among hands, objects, or environmental context, rather than reasoning from the appearance and dynamics of hands and objects themselves. To address this limitation, we propose a new learning paradigm that combines (i) hand-object masked training, which enables robust reasoning from partial hand or object observations, and (ii) an HOI-dynamics-aware decoder that explicitly learns hand- and object-centric embeddings through auxiliary predictions of their locations and semantics, enhancing sensitivity to both cues. To systematically evaluate such cue-specific reasoning, we introduce Cue-Isolated HOI (CI-HOI), a new evaluation that assesses models' ability to predict actions from hand- and object-related cues independently. To enable CI-HOI, we curate the DEHOI testbed, which separates hand- and object-related observations for disentangled HOI evaluation through inpainting. Using DEHOI, we demonstrate both quantitatively and qualitatively that our training strategy exploits hand- and object-centric information more effectively than existing models. Our approach improves over existing models on DEHOI, standard action recognition, object state recognition, and even robot manipulation action recognition, leading to more robust HOI understanding.

</details>


### [110] [Beyond wheelchairs and blindfolds: Investigating disability stereotypes in T2I models with INCLUDE-BENCH](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08515)
*Sophia Lichtenberg,Albert Gatt,Judith Masthoff*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出首个大规模评估文本到图像模型中对残疾人偏见的中文基准INCLUDE-BENCH（含119K图像），发现模型普遍将残疾简化为轮椅意象、降低生成多样性、强化刻板印象，并引入社会心理学刻板印象内容模型（SCM）评分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有T2I模型的偏见评估主要聚焦性别、肤色和文化表征，残疾维度被系统性忽视；当前评估方法缺乏社会学的刻板印象定义基础，无法对残疾人群体面临的表征危害进行原则性评估。

Method: 构建INCLUDE-BENCH基准，设计多维度歧视性提示（含静态与动态上下文），生成119K图像；评估15个开源和2个闭源模型；引入基于刻板印象内容模型（SCM）的评分体系。

Result: (1) 所有模型中，行动障碍和默认残疾提示主要生成轮椅图像；(2) 残疾条件下的生成多样性显著降低；(3) 刻板印象描绘具有更强的残疾-文本对齐；(4) SCM评分表明T2I模型反映了真实世界的刻板印象关联。

Conclusion: INCLUDE-BENCH揭示了T2I模型对残疾人群体的系统性表征偏见，SCM评分提供了一种社会学理论驱动的量化评估方法，为未来公平性研究奠定基础。

Abstract: Text-to-image (T2I) models have been shown to exhibit social biases. Prior work has mainly focused on gender, skin tone, and cultural representation within restricted occupational associations, and emerging benchmarks increasingly incorporate these dimensions. However, disability remains systematically underexplored. Current evaluation practices often fail to align with sociologically grounded definitions of stereotyping, limiting principled assessment of representational harms toward people with disabilities (PWD). To address this, we introduce INCLUDE-BENCH, the first large-scale benchmark for evaluating disability-related bias in T2I models. INCLUDE-BENCH comprises 119K generated images based on prompt design across multiple bias dimensions and both static and dynamic contexts. We evaluate 15 open-source and 2 closed-source models. Our key findings reveal that: (1) mobility-impaired and default disability prompts predominantly yield wheelchair depictions across all models; (2) disability-conditioned generations consistently exhibit less diversity; (3) stereotypical portrayals demonstrate stronger disability-text alignment; and (4) we introduce the Stereotype Content Model (SCM) Score, demonstrating that T2I models reflect real-world stereotypical associations.

</details>


### [111] [Whareformer: Learning to Track What is Where in Long Egocentric Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08537)
*Jacob Chalk,Saptarshi Sinha,Dima Damen,Yannis Kalantidis,Diane Larlus*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出首个基于学习的OSNOM任务解决方案Whareformer，使用Transformer架构联合推理外观和3D位置信息，在少量训练数据上实现SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: OSNOM任务需要跟踪被相机佩戴者移动的物体，并保持对离开视野或被严重遮挡物体的位置记忆，此前缺乏基于学习的解决方案。

Method: Whareformer由两部分组成：可更新的已建立轨迹记忆模块和轨迹分配模块；联合推理物体外观（what）和更新的3D位置（where），使用专用New Track token处理新物体，采用相对距离和演进的轨迹表示。

Result: 在56个视频上训练，在EPIC-KITCHENS-100（未见视频）、IT3DEgo和HD-EPIC三个数据集的260个长测试视频上取得SOTA性能，显著优于先前方法。

Conclusion: 基于Transformer架构和3D位置信息的联合推理是解决OSNOM任务的有效途径，少量训练数据即可获得优异的跨数据集泛化能力。

Abstract: The recently established 'Out of Sight, Not out of Mind' (OSNOM) task for egocentric videos focuses on tracking objects that are moved by the camera wearer, online, maintaining knowledge of instance locations throughout the video even when they leave the field of view or become heavily occluded. In this paper, we propose the first learning-based solution to the OSNOM task: Whareformer, a transformer-based model with two components: an updatable memory of established tracks and a track assignment module that associates observations with existing tracks in a feed-forward manner. Whareformer jointly reasons over evolving object appearance (what) and updated 3D location (where), and employs a dedicated New Track token to reason about novel objects.
  Thanks to its design choices of using relative distances and evolving track representations, Whareformer is trained on a small set of 56 videos but achieves SOTA performance on 260 long test videos from three datasets: EPIC-KITCHENS-100 (unseen videos), IT3DEgo, and HD-EPIC, with significant absolute improvements over prior work.

</details>


### [112] [VocaDet: Sample-Driven Open-Vocabulary Object Detection and Segmentation via Visual Tokenization and Vector Database Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08541)
*ZhiXin Sun*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: VocaDet提出了一种基于样本驱动的开放词汇目标检测与分割框架，通过将连续视觉表示转换为离散视觉词汇并利用向量数据库进行检索，无需模型重训练即可从用户提供的正负样本中学习目标概念，在UA-DETRAC数据集上取得了有效的检测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有开放词汇目标检测与分割方法通常依赖文本提示、有限的视觉示例或昂贵的特征匹配流程，难以扩展到大规模且持续增长的目标库。为了解决这一可扩展性问题，作者提出了一种无需模型重训练、直接从用户提供的正负样本中学习目标概念的框架。

Method: VocaDet框架包含以下关键组件：1）使用DINOv3作为视觉特征提取器；2）通过具有自适应聚类敏感性的凝聚聚类生成多粒度视觉token；3）将视觉token、位置去偏表示和空间拓扑信息作为可扩展的目标记忆存储在向量数据库中；4）推理时将查询图像转换为视觉token并与存储的目标记忆进行高效匹配；5）引入背景过滤机制以去除频繁出现的背景模式，减少固定相机场景下的冗余检索操作。

Result: 在UA-DETRAC数据集上的实验表明，VocaDet无需传统检测器训练即可实现有效的开放词汇检测性能，并且随着正负样本的积累，支持持续可扩展的识别能力。

Conclusion: VocaDet通过将视觉表示离散化为视觉词汇并借助向量数据库检索，成功地绕过了传统检测器训练的限制，实现了可扩展的开放词汇目标检测与分割，为大规模目标识别提供了一种高效且灵活的范式。

Abstract: Open-vocabulary object detection and segmentation aim to recognize arbitrary objects beyond predefined categories. Although recent vision-language and reference-based approaches have significantly advanced this field, they often rely on text prompts, limited visual examples, or expensive feature matching procedures, making them difficult to scale to large and continuously expanding object repositories. In this work, we propose VocaDet, a sample-driven open-vocabulary object detection and segmentation framework that learns object concepts directly from user-provided positive and negative sample collections without model retraining. The key idea is to transform continuous visual representations into discrete visual vocabularies and perform efficient retrieval-based recognition through a scalable vector database. Specifically, we employ DINOv3 as the visual feature extractor and apply agglomerative clustering with adaptive clustering sensitivity to generate multi-granularity visual tokens. These visual tokens, together with position-debiased representations and spatial topology information, are stored as expandable object memories in a vector database. During inference, query images are converted into visual tokens and efficiently matched against the stored object memories for object localization and segmentation. Furthermore, a background filtering mechanism is introduced to remove frequently occurring background patterns and reduce redundant retrieval operations in practical fixed-camera scenarios. Experiments on the UA-DETRAC dataset demonstrate that VocaDet achieves effective open-vocabulary detection performance without conventional detector training, while supporting continuously expandable recognition capability as additional positive and negative samples are accumulated.

</details>


### [113] [Switch-Reasoner: Learn When to Think in Multitask Mixtures via Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08572)
*Yiyang Fang,Pei Fu,Jinjie Li,Jian Liang,Wenke Huang,Ruijie Luo,Shaojie Zhang,Jian Luan,Yi R. Fung,Mang Ye*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Switch-Reasoner框架，基于GRPO让MLLM自适应选择推理模式（直接回答或先推理后回答），通过双层调控机制平衡两种模式，在11个多模态任务上实现了更好的准确率-效率权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MLLM采用固定的"先思考再回答"范式，在异构多任务场景中效率低下——简单输入不需要显式推理，而困难输入能从推理中获益，但模型难以自适应地决定何时需要推理，后训练中也不稳定。

Method: 基于GRPO的框架，将思考视为虚拟工具调用，模型可选择直接回答或调用推理后再回答。引入双层调控机制：全局层面平衡两种模式的使用比例，样本层面根据两种选择的相对收益提供监督信号。

Result: 在11个多模态任务上的实验表明，Switch-Reasoner减少了不必要的推理，同时保持了强性能，实现了更好的准确率-效率权衡。

Conclusion: 通过自适应推理模式选择，可以有效解决MLLM在异构多任务场景中的效率问题，避免"总是思考"或"总是直接回答"的极端行为，实现性能与效率的更优平衡。

Abstract: Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) often follow a fixed Think-then-Answer paradigm, which is inefficient in heterogeneous multitask settings because simple inputs may not require explicit reasoning while difficult ones can benefit substantially from it. Learning when to think is also unstable during post-training, where imbalanced rollouts can drive the model toward always-thinking or always-direct behavior. We propose Switch-Reasoner, a GRPO-based framework that learns to adaptively select reasoning modes for MLLMs. It treats thinking as a virtual tool invocation and allows the model to either answer directly or invoke explicit reasoning before answering. To stabilize this decision, we introduce a dual-level regulation mechanism that balances the overall use of Thinking Mode and Direct Mode while providing sample-level supervision based on the relative benefit of the two choices. Experiments on 11 multimodal tasks show that Switch-Reasoner reduces unnecessary reasoning while maintaining strong performance, achieving a better accuracy-efficiency trade-off.

</details>


### [114] [When Structured Sparse Autoencoders Learn Consistent Concepts Across Modalities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08605)
*Weiduo Liao,Yunqiao Yang,Ying Wei*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 针对视觉-语言模型中稀疏自编码器（SAE）在视觉模态难以学习模态一致概念的问题，提出结构化稀疏自编码器（S²AE），通过基于注意力相似性和空间邻近性对图像块进行分组，并引入由组间排他稀疏和组内群体稀疏组成的结构化稀疏正则化，在Qwen2.5-VL-7B-Instruct模型上实现了语义对齐（mIoU）平均提升6.06%、表征效率（l0范数）提升60.81，同时保持99%以上的解释方差，跨模态分析表明该方法增强了神经元的多语义性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在视觉-语言模型中，传统稀疏自编码器（SAE）难以学习到模态一致的概念，视觉模态中的概念常表现出碎片化覆盖（即不连续的区域），导致学到的潜在特征缺乏跨模态一致性。

Method: 提出结构化稀疏自编码器（S²AE）：(1) 基于Transformer注意力相似性和空间邻近性对图像块进行分组；(2) 引入结构化稀疏正则化，包括组间排他稀疏（促进不同组间概念解耦）和组内群体稀疏（促进组内概念一致性），驱动SAE的潜在神经元专注于不同且语义一致的概念。

Result: 在Qwen2.5-VL-7B-Instruct上：语义对齐（mIoU）平均提升6.06%；表征效率（l0范数）降低60.81；重建保真度保持99%以上解释方差；跨模态语义一致性平均提升3.08%；神经元多语义性得分平均提升2.37%。

Conclusion: 通过引入视觉结构先验（空间和语义分组），S²AE有效增强了多模态特征中神经元的单语义性，促进了更连贯和更具解耦性的多模态表征学习，解决了视觉-语言模型中概念碎片化和模态不一致的问题。

Abstract: Sparse autoencoders (SAEs) have emerged as a promising technique for mechanistic interpretability by learning a set of sparse latent features in large models, each of which encodes a distinct concept. However, in vision-language models (VLMs), vanilla SAEs struggle to learn modality-consistent concepts, with concepts often exhibiting fragmented coverage (i.e., disjoint regions) in the visual modality. To address this challenge, we propose a Structured Sparse AutoEncoder ($S^2AE$) that enforces concept consistency from both semantic and spatial perspectives in the visual modality. Specifically, we group image patches based on Transformer attention similarity and spatial proximity, and introduce a structured sparsity regularization when training the vanilla SAE. The regularization consists of exclusive sparsity for inter-group concept disentanglement and group sparsity for intra-group concept consistency, which drives the latent neurons by SAEs to specialize in distinct, semantically grounded concepts. Evaluated on the \texttt{Qwen2.5-VL-7B-Instruct} model, the method achieves 6.06% average improvement in semantic alignment (mIoU) and 60.81 in representational efficiency (lower l0 norm) while maintaining near-perfect reconstruction fidelity with an Explained Variance above 99%. Cross-modal analysis further demonstrates that $S^2AE$ enhances neuronal monosemanticity by this visual structural prior, achieving a 3.08% average gain in semantic consistency and a 2.37% average gain in monosemanticity scores for both modalities of multimodal features, thereby fostering more coherent and disentangled representations.

</details>


### [115] [Do Transformations Reveal the Truth? Generative Residual Learning for Generalized AI-Generated Image Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08674)
*Kutub Uddin,Nusrat Tasnim,Awais Khan,Mohammad Umar Farooq,Khalid Malik*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出GenRes和GenRes++框架，通过神经张量网络和可学习注意力机制进行生成式残差学习，用于检测AI生成图像，在多个基准数据集上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式AI的快速发展使得创建高度逼真的深度伪造媒体成为可能，带来了虚假信息、数字身份盗窃、欺诈和舆论操纵等重大威胁。由于生成方法的多样性和伪影的微妙性，AI生成图像检测仍然可靠地具有挑战性。

Method: 首先提出GenRes框架，通过神经张量网络进行生成式残差学习，建模原始样本与变换样本之间的细粒度关系特征以增强泛化能力。针对多重生成变换场景，进一步提出GenRes++，采用可学习注意力机制聚合多个变换样本的关系特征，使模型关注最具信息量的线索。两个模型均使用PE-Core作为特征提取器，提供泛化且语义丰富的嵌入。

Result: 在多个基准数据集上的综合实验表明，所提出的GenRes++方法在性能上优于现有方法。

Conclusion: GenRes和GenRes++框架通过关系特征建模和注意力机制有效提升了AI生成图像检测的泛化能力和跨域性能，能够检测未见生成方法产生的AIGI。

Abstract: The rapid advancement of generative AI has enabled the creation of highly realistic deepfake media, posing significant threats, including misinformation, digital identity theft, fraud, and manipulation of public opinion. AI-generated image (AIGI) detection is reliably challenging due to the diversity of generative methods and the subtle artifacts they leave behind. In this work, we propose GenRes, a novel framework for generative residual learning via a neural tensor network, which models fine-grained relational features between original and transformed samples to enhance generalization. To address scenarios involving multiple generative transformations, we introduce GenRes++, which employs a learnable attention mechanism to aggregate relational features across multiple transformed samples and enables the model to focus on the most informative cues. Both models leverage PE-Core as a feature extractor, providing generalized and semantically rich embeddings that improve cross-domain performance and enable the detection of AIGI generated by unseen methods. Comprehensive experiments on multiple benchmark datasets demonstrate that the proposed GenRes++ approach outperforms existing methods.

</details>


### [116] [Multi-Resolution Feature Stem for Diabetic Retinopathy lesion segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08679)
*Indranil Dutta,Taehee Jeong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究了糖尿病视网膜病变分割中输入分辨率对不同病变类型（微小动脉瘤和大出血）的相反影响，并提出了一种多分辨率特征茎（Multi-Resolution Feature Stem）架构来解决这一分辨率权衡问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DR病变从微小动脉瘤到大出血/渗出物尺寸差异巨大，这给模型架构和输入分辨率设计带来冲突性需求，高分辨率是否始终有益尚不清楚。

Method: 系统实验了U-Net、UNet++、Vision Transformers、DeepLabV3+等架构在512×512和1024×1024分辨率下的效果；提出多分辨率特征茎（Multi-Resolution Feature Stem）作为输入级金字塔与UNet++骨干结合。

Result: 高分辨率对微小动脉瘤的分割是必要的，但会意外地降低大出血病变的分割性能；所提方法在保持上下文信息的同时捕捉细粒度细节，有效解决了分辨率权衡。

Conclusion: 提供了分辨率依赖性行为的关键实证证据，并提出了一种参数高效、实用的架构来解决不同病变类型之间的分辨率冲突。

Abstract: Diabetic Retinopathy (DR) is a leading cause of preventable blindness worldwide, requiring automated lesion segmentation using deep learning models for early detection and monitoring. However, DR lesions vary dramatically in size from tiny microaneurysms to large hemorrhages and exudates. This variability creates conflicting demands on the model architecture and input resolution, posing a challenge for effective design. This work investigates the impact of input resolution on different lesion types. Through systematic experimentation with multiple architectures (U-Net, UNet++, Vision Transformers, DeepLabV3+) at $512 \times 512$ and $1024 \times 1024$ resolutions, we identify a critical, counter-intuitive phenomenon where increasing input resolution has opposing effects on different lesion types. We demonstrate that while higher resolution is essential for resolving fine-grained microaneurysms, it can unexpectedly degrade performance on larger hemorrhages. This finding challenges the common assumption that higher resolution is uniformly beneficial. To address this, we propose a novel Multi-Resolution Feature Stem, an input-level pyramid integrated with a UNet++ backbone. This architecture processes multiple scales in parallel, capturing fine-grained details without sacrificing contextual information. This work contributes crucial empirical evidence of this complex, resolution-dependent behavior and a practical, parameter-efficient architecture that successfully resolves this trade-off.

</details>


### [117] [SAM-MT: Real-Time Interactive Multi-Target Video Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08688)
*Ruiqi Shen,Chang Liu,Henghui Ding*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SAM-MT基于SAM2改造，通过解耦目标数量与延迟，实现了多目标视频分割的实时处理（10目标>36 FPS），采用显式查询、解耦掩码注意力、稀疏记忆等策略保持性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VOS方法在多目标场景下需为每个目标复制单目标处理流程，导致帧率下降且延迟随目标数量线性增长，无法满足实时需求。

Method: 基于SAM2提出SAM-MT，采用显式查询表示不同目标并共享全局上下文，使用解耦掩码注意力保持目标身份独立、防止交叉干扰，引入稀疏记忆实现稳定时序演化，并设计遮挡处理和重叠防止策略。

Result: 成功解耦延迟与目标数量，10个目标时达到>36 FPS的实时速度，与单目标基线相当，同时保持SAM2的鲁棒分割性能。

Conclusion: SAM-MT有效解决了多目标VOS的实时性问题，在不牺牲性能的前提下实现了与目标数量无关的恒定低延迟。

Abstract: Modern Video Object Segmentation (VOS) involves tracking and segmenting user-specified targets. While recent approaches have achieved remarkable performance in single-target scenarios, extending them to multi-target settings typically involves replicating the single-target processing for each individual object, resulting in reduced frame rates (FPS) with unbounded latency as target count increases. Built upon Segment Anything 2 (SAM2), we propose SAM-MT, which addresses this by transforming the model into an interactive framework for real-time Multi-Target video segmentation. SAM-MT uses explicit queries to represent different individual targets, in parallel with a shared representation for global context. It employs decoupled masked attention to keep individual identities distinct from cross-target interference, and sparse memory for stable temporal evolution, along with specialized strategies for occlusion handling and overlap prevention. SAM-MT successfully decouples latency from the number of targets, achieving real-time speed on par with single-target baselines (>36 FPS for 10 targets) while maintaining SAM2's robust video segmentation performance.

</details>


### [118] [HumanForge: A Human-Centric Deepfake Video Benchmark with Multi-Agent Forgery Rationales](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08705)
*Wenbo Xu,Zhimin Chen,Xiaojie Liang,Hengrui Liu,Wei Lu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出HumanForge统一大规模多范式人类中心视频伪造数据集，以及Gen2Anno自动化多智能体标注管线，通过18K+高保真视频片段和结构化对比全标注，揭示了当前伪造检测方法在零样本泛化和细粒度推理上的显著挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准主要关注换脸或全局文本到视频合成，忽略了人-物/人-人交互以及多模态对齐等关键维度，亟需统一的、覆盖多范式的人类中心视频伪造数据集。此外，手工标注成本高且幻觉性提示不可靠，需要自动化的高质量标注方案。

Method: 提出HumanForge数据集和Gen2Anno管线。Gen2Anno是基于LangGraph的模块化主动多智能体系统，协调六个专用智能体（来源分析、MoE参考分析、闭环取证验证等），自动生成18K+高保真视频段及结构化对比全标注（包含二元决策、细粒度伪造类别和时空定位）。

Result: 基于SOTA传统检测器和大规模多模态模型（LMMs）的广泛基准测试表明，HumanForge在零样本泛化和细粒度推理任务上对现有方法构成显著挑战。

Conclusion: HumanForge填补了人类中心视频伪造检测中交互维度缺失的空白，Gen2Anno实现了高效自动化标注。数据集和代码将公开，为数字取证领域提供重要资源。

Abstract: Rapid advancements in video diffusion models and temporal editing tools have enabled the generation of highly realistic human-centric videos, posing unprecedented challenges to digital content forensics. Existing benchmarks primarily focus on either face-swapping or global text-to-video synthesis, overlooking the crucial dimensions of human-object or human-human interactions and multi-modal alignment. To address these limitations, we introduce HumanForge, a unified, large-scale, and multi-paradigm human-centric video forgery dataset. To construct and annotate this dataset without labor-intensive manual labeling or hallucinated monolithic prompts, we propose Gen2Anno, a modular active multi-agent pipeline built on LangGraph. Gen2Anno coordinates six specialized agents-ranging from source profiling to MoE-based reference analysis and closed-loop forensic verification-to generate over 18K high-fidelity video segments and produce structured, contrastive omni-annotations containing binary decisions, fine-grained artifact categories, and spatio-temporal localization. Extensive benchmarks using state-of-the-art traditional detectors and Large Multimodal Models (LMMs) demonstrate the significant challenges of zero-shot generalization and fine-grained reasoning on HumanForge. Code and dataset will be publicly released.

</details>


### [119] [LTM: Large-scale Terrain Model for Wildfire-prone Landscapes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08711)
*Xiao Fu,Yue Hu,Meida Chen,Peter Anthony Beerel,Barath Raghavan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种多模态重建框架，利用过时的数字高程模型（DEM）作为几何先验，通过物理基础的像素-像素对齐实现图像与DEM数据的匹配，在保持实时性能的同时生成高保真深度图，显著提升 wildfire 场景下三维地形重建的精度和计算效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 野火多发区域范围广阔，传统三维重建方法表现不佳；机载LiDAR成本高昂且更新频率低；基于图像的方法受限于稀疏视觉特征和有限的图像重叠。

Method: 提出多模态重建框架，利用过时DEM作为几何先验；核心创新是物理基础的像素-像素对齐，将图像与DEM数据直接匹配，消除昂贵的特征匹配流程。

Result: 在基于真实 wildfire 区域的大地形模拟器上验证，该方法在给定带位姿的图像和历史DEM数据后，能生成高保真深度图并保持实时性能，重建精度和计算效率显著优于现有技术。

Conclusion: 该方法为 wildfire 响应提供了一种可扩展的解决方案，在保证实时性的同时实现了高精度地形重建。

Abstract: Accurate 3D terrain maps are essential for emergency response when assessing wildfire hazards. However, wildfire-prone regions often span vast areas where conventional reconstruction methods underperform. Airborne LiDAR systems provide high-resolution terrain data, but they are expensive and infrequently updated. Image-based methods offer a lower-cost alternative, but struggle due to sparse visual features and limited image overlap. We propose a multi-modal reconstruction framework leveraging outdated Digital Elevation Models (DEMs) as geometric priors for image-based 3D reconstruction. Our key innovation is physics-based pixel-pixel alignment between images and DEM data, dramatically reducing computational complexity by eliminating expensive feature matching procedures. To validate our approach, we developed a large-terrain simulator based on a real wildfire-prone area, generating realistic images enabling a comprehensive evaluation. Given posed images and legacy DEMs, our method produces high-fidelity depth maps while maintaining real-time performance. We find significant improvements in reconstruction accuracy and computational efficiency over existing techniques, offering a scalable solution for wildfire response.

</details>


### [120] [Pose-to-Biomechanics: Bridging 3D Human Pose Estimation and Biomechanical Attribute Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08725)
*Ayda Eghbalian,Kevin Desai*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出BioModule，一种轻量级插件式时间Transformer，可接入任意3D姿态估计器下游，从标准17关节3D骨架预测生物力学属性，实现从视觉姿态估计到生物力学运动分析的模块化桥接。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3D人体姿态估计主要优化几何关键点精度，但康复、运动科学、工效学等实际应用需要描述身体如何运动、负载和激活的生物力学量，而现有姿态估计器缺乏对此类物理可解释运动分析的直接支持。

Method: 提出BioModule，一种轻量级插件式时间Transformer，可附加在任何3D姿态估计器下游，无需修改上游模型；构建了Human3.6M视频/3D关键点与Human3.6Mplus生物力学标签空间的大规模对齐数据集，并建立解剖坐标系对应关系实现逐帧跨模态监督。

Result: 在7种最先进的3D姿态估计器上进行了系统基准测试，首次分析了上游姿态估计质量如何传播到下游生物力学预测保真度。

Conclusion: BioModule作为紧凑、模块化的桥梁，连接了视觉姿态估计与生物力学意义上的人体运动分析，使现有姿态估计器能够扩展至物理可解释的运动分析。

Abstract: Recent progress in 3D human pose estimation has made markerless recovery of skeletal motion increasingly accurate and scalable. However, most pose estimators remain optimized for geometric keypoint accuracy, while many real-world applications in rehabilitation, sports science, ergonomics, and clinical movement analysis require biomechanical quantities that describe how the body moves, loads, and activates. In this work, we propose BioModule, a lightweight plug-in temporal transformer that attaches downstream of any 3D pose estimator and predicts biomechanical attributes from standard 17-joint 3D skeletons. BioModule is estimator-agnostic and requires no modification of the upstream pose model, enabling existing pose estimators to be extended toward physically interpretable motion analysis.
  To train and evaluate BioModule, we construct a large-scale aligned dataset pairing Human3.6M video and 3D keypoints with the biomechanical label space of Human3.6Mplus. We establish and verify anatomical correspondence between coordinate systems of the two datasets, enabling frame-accurate cross-modal supervision. Using this aligned supervision, BioModule predicts biomechanical quantities. We further benchmark BioModule across seven state-of-the-art 3D pose estimators, providing the first systematic analysis of how upstream pose estimation quality propagates to downstream biomechanical prediction fidelity. The results position BioModule as a compact, modular bridge between vision-based pose estimation and biomechanically meaningful human motion analysis.

</details>


### [121] [WaspMOT: A Benchmark for Long-Term Multi-Object Tracking of Trichogramma Wasps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08729)
*Tomasz Stanczyk,Yuan Gao,Hardik Agarwal,Seongroo Yoon,Tiantao Zhang,Vincent Calcagno,Francois Bremond*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: WaspMOT 是一个长时多目标跟踪基准，包含约12,000帧/序列的赤眼蜂跟踪数据（共10个序列），在封闭场景下评估方法的长期身份保持能力。实验发现现有方法即使使用完美检测也存在严重轨迹碎片化问题，空间轨迹拼接基线可稳定提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MOT基准以短视频序列为主，无法充分评估长时身份保持能力。本文旨在填补这一空白，通过长时封闭场景的昆虫跟踪数据，暴露传统数据集无法观测的长期关联问题。

Method: 构建WaspMOT基准：10个序列，每序列约12,000帧（25FPS下超8分钟），含密集MOTChallenge标注和Oracle检测。在统一协议下评估ByteTrack、BoT-SORT、C-BIoU、OC-SORT、McByte五种跟踪-by-检测方法，并提出空间轨迹拼接基线。

Result: 所有方法在完美检测条件下仍出现显著轨迹碎片化，空间轨迹拼接基线可一致提升性能，表明长时身份保持仍存在巨大改进空间。

Conclusion: WaspMOT揭示了现有跟踪方法在长时关联上的根本局限，为研究长期身份保持提供了新的评估平台，证明现有方法在该任务上有实质性的提升潜力。

Abstract: Multi-object tracking (MOT) has achieved strong performance on benchmarks dominated by short video sequences. However, such datasets do not adequately evaluate long-term identity preservation, where objects must be tracked consistently over extended durations. We introduce WaspMOT, a benchmark designed to address this gap through long-duration tracking of Trichogramma wasps in controlled ecological experiments. The dataset contains 10 sequences of approximately 12,000 frames each (over 8 minutes at 25 FPS), with dense MOTChallenge annotations and oracle detections to isolate association performance.
  Unlike existing benchmarks, WaspMOT forms a closed-set tracking scenario where all individuals remain present throughout the sequence, requiring consistent identity assignment across thousands of frames despite abrupt jumps, occlusions, and highly similar appearance. We establish a benchmark by evaluating five tracking-by-detection methods, including ByteTrack, BoT-SORT, C-BIoU, OC-SORT, and McByte, under a unified protocol. Results show that all methods suffer from significant trajectory fragmentation, highlighting the difficulty of long-term identity preservation even with perfect detections. A simple spatial tracklet stitching baseline consistently improves performance, indicating that substantial gains remain possible.
  WaspMOT provides a new benchmark for studying long-term association and reveals limitations of current tracking approaches that are not observable on conventional datasets. The benchmark will be made publicly available at the project repository: https://github.com/tstanczyk95/WaspMOT/ .

</details>


### [122] [OpenCoF: Learning to Reason Through Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08763)
*Xinyan Chen,Ziyu Guo,Renrui Zhang,Dongzhi Jiang,Hongsheng Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出OpenCoF框架，包含OpenCoF-17K推理视频数据集（覆盖11个任务族）和基于Wan2.2微调的Wan-CoF模型，通过时间关联帧（Chain-of-Frame）实现推理，并引入视觉和文本推理token来增强时空推理能力，在四个视频推理基准上取得显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频生成模型主要在通用视频语料上训练，缺乏针对Chain-of-Frame推理的多样化监督和专用设计，无法有效支持通过时间连接帧进行推理的需求。

Method: 1) 构建OpenCoF-17K数据集，覆盖11个任务族的推理视频数据；2) 在Wan2.2-I2V-A14B基础上微调得到Wan-CoF模型；3) 引入视觉推理token和文本推理token，分别捕获低级视觉线索和高级语义先验，用于空间与时间推理。

Result: Wan-CoF在四个视频推理基准上相比Wan2.2-I2V-A14B基线获得显著提升；通过性能对比和注意力分析，验证了推理token在模型深度、去噪步数、空间和时间维度上的贡献。

Conclusion: 更强的视频推理能力需要广泛的时空监督和显式的中间推理状态组织机制。已开源数据集、模型和代码以促进推理导向视频生成的未来研究。

Abstract: Reasoning has become a core capability for large models, especially when reliable decisions require understanding logical consequences. Recent video generation models offer a reasoning path distinct from previous Chain-of-Thought (CoT): reasoning can unfold through temporally connected frames, known as Chain-of-Frame (CoF) reasoning. However, existing video generators are primarily trained on general video corpora, still lacking diverse supervision and dedicated designs for CoF reasoning. To address this gap, we introduce OpenCoF, a framework comprising the OpenCoF-17K dataset, a reasoning video dataset spanning 11 task families, and Wan-CoF, a fine-tuned video model for studying whether diverse temporal supervision improves CoF behavior. Across four video reasoning benchmarks, Wan-CoF achieves considerable gains over the Wan2.2-I2V-A14B baseline. Building on this, we empirically explore more advanced designs for CoF capabilities, i.e., equipping the model with visual and textual reasoning tokens. This mechanism respectively captures low-level visual cues and high-level semantic priors for spatial and temporal reasoning. Through performance comparisons and attention analysis, we examine how these tokens contribute across model depth, denoising steps, space, and time. Our results suggest that stronger video reasoning requires both broad temporal supervision and explicit mechanisms for organizing intermediate reasoning state. We open-source the dataset, model, and code to facilitate future research on reasoning-oriented video generation.

</details>


### [123] [Enhancing In-context Panoramic Generation via Geometric-aware Pretraining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08765)
*Haoran Feng,Ruiyang Zhang,Longyi Zhang,Dizhe Zhang,Lu Qi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Canvas360是一个两阶段全景生成框架，结合几何感知预训练和任务特定微调，并引入包含100万高质量配对样本的Canvas360Dataset，通过并行深度生成、速度循环填充和相似性损失正则化等技术，在多个下游任务中实现了优于先前方法的全景图像生成效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有全景生成方法缺乏大规模高质量训练数据，且难以在统一框架下同时支持风格迁移、修补、外推和编辑等多种上下文感知生成任务。

Method: 提出两阶段框架：1) 几何感知预训练阶段，通过并行深度生成、速度循环填充和相似性损失正则化学习几何感知表示；2) 下游任务微调阶段，利用token级拼接实现统一框架。同时构建了包含100万高质量配对样本的Canvas360Dataset。

Result: 在全景特定的FAED指标上表现优异，在多项定量评估中取得竞争力或领先结果，全景图像保真度显著提升。

Conclusion: Canvas360通过几何感知预训练和大规模数据集，实现了统一且灵活的全景上下文生成框架，在任务覆盖度和建模灵活性上超越先前方法。

Abstract: In this work, we present Canvas360, a two-stage framework for in-context panoramic generation that combines geometry-aware pretraining with downstream task-specific fine-tuning. To address the lack of large-scale, high-quality training data tailored to in-context panoramic tasks, we propose Canvas360Dataset, a collection of 1M high-quality paired panoramic samples for style transfer, inpainting, outpainting, and editing, enabling effective supervision across diverse in-context generation scenarios. On the modeling side, Canvas360 enhances text-to-panorama generation through parallel depth generation, velocity circular padding, and similarity loss regularization, enabling the model to learn geometry-aware representations, capture object distortion details, and improve geometric consistency and global coherence. Furthermore, empowered by strong panoramic priors, Canvas360 enables a unified in-context panoramic generation framework that supports diverse downstream tasks via token-level concatenation, surpassing prior methods in both task coverage and modeling flexibility. Extensive experiments show that Canvas360 improves panoramic image fidelity, achieving particularly strong performance on the panorama-specific FAED metric and competitive or leading results across the reported quantitative evaluations. More information can be found on our project page: https://zry000.github.io/Canvas360/

</details>


### [124] [OPSD-V: On-Policy Self-Distillation for Post-Training Few-Step Autoregressive Video Generators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08766)
*Hongyu Liu,Chun Wang,Feng Gao,Xuanhua He,Yue Ma,Ziyu Wan,Yong Zhang,Xiaoming Wei,Qifeng Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出OPSD-V，一种在策略自蒸馏范式，用于后训练少步自回归视频扩散模型，通过引入真实长视频作为时序上下文提供稠密轨迹级监督，在不改变推理路径条件下减少长程退化，提升视觉质量和运动动力学。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有少步AR视频生成器能生成长视频但存在误差累积和运动动态弱化问题，需要在不改变少步推理路径的前提下减少长程退化。

Method: 学生模型按推理时 rollout 逐块生成（基于自身KV缓存），教师模型在同一去噪状态但使用更干净的AR-consistent时序缓存（真实视频替换旧历史），提供稠密的去噪级校正目标，实现在策略AR缓存动态下的自蒸馏。

Result: 在Self-Forcing和LongLive等模型上实验，视觉质量、运动动态和VBenchLong评分一致提升；用户研究中66.0%偏好OPSD-V（排除平局后82.5%）。

Conclusion: OPSD-V能有效改善少步AR视频生成的长程退化问题，且不改变采样器、去噪步数或推理时缓存机制。

Abstract: We propose OPSD-V, an on-policy self-distillation paradigm for post-training few-step autoregressive (AR) video diffusion models. Existing few-step AR video generators can produce long videos with low latency, but still suffer from error accumulation and weakened motion dynamics during long autoregressive rollout. OPSD-V reduces long-horizon degradation while preserving the original few-step inference path. The key idea is to introduce real long-video data as temporal context during training and use it to provide dense trajectory-level supervision. Specifically, the student follows the exact inference-time rollout, generating each chunk conditioned on its own previously generated KV cache. In parallel, the teacher is evaluated at the same student-visited denoising states, but uses a cleaner AR-consistent temporal cache in which older history can be replaced by real-video context. This provides dense denoising-level corrective targets under on-policy AR cache dynamics, without changing the sampler, number of denoising steps, or inference-time cache mechanism. We apply OPSD-V to representative few-step AR video models, including Self-Forcing and LongLive. Experiments show consistent improvements in visual quality, motion dynamics, and VBenchLong scores. A user study with 10 participants comparing 20 video pairs shows that OPSD-V is preferred over the base models in 66.0% of overall-preference judgments (82.5% excluding ties).

</details>


### [125] [Geometry and Gradient-based Partitioning for Panoramic Outdoor Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08769)
*Weijian Chen,Weibo Yao,Yuhang Zhang,Xiaolin Tang,Guo Wang,Weijun Zhang,Xitong Gao,Yihao Chen,Hongde Qin,Lu Qi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出PanoLOG框架，通过几何与梯度引导的分区策略（G²PS），实现大尺度户外场景的全景3DGS高效重建，并发布了首个大尺度全景户外重建基准Pano360。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将3DGS扩展到大规模户外场景在数据采集和计算上成本高昂；采用全景图像虽能减少采集开销，但其全向可见性使得现有基于局部相机视锥的分区策略失效，导致块优化退化为全局训练，亟需一种适配全景图像的大规模3DGS分区重建方法。

Method: 提出两阶段粗到细框架PanoLOG：全局粗阶段利用天空球建模和全景单目深度监督获得可靠几何；细化阶段提出G²PS（几何与梯度引导分区策略），通过视差驱动的不确定性构建自适应包围体，并基于梯度重要性评分分配相机。

Result: 在构建的Pano360基准数据集上，G²PS实现了最先进的渲染质量，同时保持了可扩展的块并行训练能力。

Conclusion: 本文证明了通过合理的几何与梯度引导分区策略，全景图像可以有效支持大规模户外场景的3DGS高效重建，为后续研究提供了数据集和开源代码。

Abstract: Scaling 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) to large outdoor scenes is costly in both data acquisition and computation. Adopting panoramic images with equirectangular projection (ERP) can reduce capture effort via their full $360^{\circ}$ field of view, yet the resulting omnipresent visibility invalidates existing partitioning strategies that rely on local camera frustums, causing block-wise optimization to degenerate into global training. Thus, we propose PanoLOG, a two-stage coarse-to-fine framework equipped with a Geometry and Gradient-based Partitioning Strategy tailored for large-scale panoramic 3DGS reconstruction. In the global coarse stage, PanoLOG leverages sky-sphere modeling and panoramic monocular depth supervision for reliable geometry, while in the refinement stage, G$^2$PS builds adaptive bounding volumes via parallax-driven uncertainty and assigns cameras via gradient-based importance scoring. Furthermore, we construct Pano360, the first benchmark on large-scale panoramic dataset for outdoor scene reconstruction. Extensive experiments demonstrate that G$^2$PS achieves state-of-the-art rendering quality while maintaining scalable, block-parallel training. Our models, training code, and dataset are publicly available.

</details>


### [126] [LongE2V: Long-Horizon Event-based Video Reconstruction, Prediction, and Frame Interpolation with Video Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08770)
*Cheng-De Fan,Chun-Wei Tuan Mu,Chen-Wei Chang,Chin-Yang Lin,Kun-Ru Wu,Yu-Chee Tseng,Yu-Lun Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: LongE2V利用预训练的视频扩散先验，通过自回归展开、自适应上下文切换、重编码对齐与交叉残差校正、事件体素密度增强等技术，在事件相机视频重建、预测和帧插值任务上实现了优于现有方法的性能，具有高数据效率、优越感知质量、长时间稳定性及零样本泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从稀疏事件流中恢复高质量视频具有挑战性，回归方法常导致纹理模糊，现有生成模型缺乏长期稳定性，因此需要一种能同时处理事件视频重建、预测和帧插值的高质量方法。

Method: 1) 微调预训练视频扩散模型作为基础；2) 自回归展开与自适应上下文切换解决长时间序列的时间漂移；3) 重编码对齐与交叉残差校正保证帧插值的双向一致性；4) 事件体素密度增强提升对不同传感器分辨率的鲁棒性。

Result: 在真实世界基准上，LongE2V在事件视频重建、预测和帧插值三个任务上均优于现有最先进方法，展现出卓越的时间一致性和零样本泛化能力。

Conclusion: LongE2V通过结合视频扩散先验与多项时序一致性技术，有效解决了事件到视频转换中的长期稳定性与高质量重建问题，为事件视频处理提供了统一且高效的解决方案。

Abstract: Recovering high-quality video from sparse event streams is a challenging task. Regression methods often blur textures, while existing generative models struggle with long-term stability. We propose LongE2V, a novel approach that leverages pre-trained video diffusion priors to jointly handle event-based video reconstruction, prediction, and frame interpolation. By fine-tuning a foundational video model, our approach achieves high data efficiency and superior perceptual quality. We introduce Autoregressive Unrolling and Adaptive Context Switching to mitigate temporal drift in extremely long sequences. We also propose Reencoding Alignment with Cross Residual Correction to ensure precise bidirectional consistency during frame interpolation. Furthermore, Event Voxel Density Augmentation ensures robustness across varying sensor resolutions. Extensive experiments on real-world benchmarks demonstrate that LongE2V outperforms state-of-the-art methods across all three tasks, exhibiting exceptional temporal coherence and zero-shot generalization. Project page: https://cdfan0627.github.io/LongE2V-page/

</details>


### [127] [ZipDepth: Bringing Lightweight Zero-Shot Monocular Depth Anywhere, on Any Device](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08771)
*Fabio Tosi,Luca Bartolomei,Matteo Poggi,Stefano Mattoccia*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ZipDepth是一个仅6.1M参数的轻量级单目深度估计网络，通过高效重参数化编码器-解码器架构和大规模多域知识蒸馏，在保证实时推理能力的同时，实现了接近50倍参数量的基础模型的零样本泛化精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基础模型零样本泛化能力强但计算开销巨大，无法部署在嵌入式/移动平台；而轻量级模型仅在单域自监督范式下开发，跨域场景下精度急剧下降。亟需一种兼顾零样本精度与部署效率的轻量级解决方案。

Method: 1) 设计高效的可重参数化编码器-解码器结构；2) 从大规模基础模型中进行知识蒸馏，利用多域训练集提供监督信号；3) 整体模型仅6.1M参数。

Result: 在5个基准上达到轻量级模型中最优的零样本精度与部署效率权衡；从服务器GPU到功耗受限设备均可实时运行；以50倍少的参数实现了接近基础模型的精度。

Conclusion: ZipDepth通过高效的架构设计与多域知识蒸馏，成功弥合了轻量级模型与基础模型之间的零样本泛化能力差距，为资源受限平台上的单目深度估计提供了实用方案。

Abstract: Monocular depth estimation has seen remarkable progress through foundation models achieving robust zero-shot generalization, yet their computational demands place them far beyond the reach of embedded and mobile platforms. Lightweight alternatives exist, but have been developed almost exclusively within single-domain, self-supervised paradigms, failing silently under domain shift. We present ZipDepth, a compact monocular depth network that bridges this gap by combining an efficient reparameterizable encoder-decoder with large-scale knowledge distillation from a foundation model over a large multi-domain training set. Comprising just 6.1M parameters, ZipDepth runs at real-time rates from server GPUs to power-constrained devices, achieving the best trade-off between zero-shot accuracy and deployment efficiency among lightweight models across five benchmarks, taking a significant step towards the accuracy of foundation models with 50x more parameters.

</details>


### [128] [Wat3R: Underwater 3D Geometry Learning without Annotations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08772)
*Jiangwei Ren,Xingyu Jiang,Zijie Song,Wei Xu,Hongkai Lin,Dingkang Liang,Xiang Bai*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Wat3R是一种跨域半监督学习框架，无需水下标注数据，通过教师-学生架构和跨视图一致性损失，将前馈3D重建模型从空气域适应到水下场景，显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 水下环境存在光衰减、散射等挑战，且缺乏大规模高质量3D标注数据，现有依赖密集标注的方法在水下场景中不切实际。

Method: 采用教师-学生半监督架构，仅利用未标注的真实水下视频数据学习鲁棒的几何表示；设计跨视图一致性损失，利用多视图几何线索补偿当前视图因水介质衰减和散射造成的信息退化；并构建了Water3D评估基准数据集。

Result: 在水下多视图深度估计和点云重建任务中，Wat3R全面超越当前最先进方法。

Conclusion: 基于未标注水下视频的跨域半监督学习策略能有效克服水下3D重建的标注瓶颈和介质干扰问题，为水下场景几何估计提供了可行框架。

Abstract: Estimating 3D geometry in underwater environments presents unique challenges due to light attenuation, scattering, and the absence of large-scale, high-quality 3D annotations. Pioneering methods rely on massive dense annotations that are impractical in underwater settings. In this paper, we propose Wat3R, a cross-domain semi-supervised learning framework designed to adapt feed-forward 3D reconstruction models from air to underwater scenes. Uniquely, our method eliminates the need for any annotated underwater data following a teacher-student architecture, that learns robust geometry representations merely on abundant unlabeled real underwater video footage. We also design a cross-view consistency loss that leverages geometric cues from other views to compensate for the information degradation in the current view caused by water attenuation and scattering. Furthermore, considering the lack of comprehensive evaluation benchmarks, we construct Water3D, a diverse dataset covering various water bodies and underwater scenarios, designed for geometric task evaluation. Experimental results demonstrate that Wat3R outperforms current state-of-the-art methods in underwater multi-view depth estimation and point cloud reconstruction. The dataset and code are available at https://github.com/LSXI7/Wat3R .

</details>


<div id='cs.CR'></div>

# cs.CR [[Back]](#toc)

### [129] [Controllability-Aware Adversarial Examples Against LLM-Based Network Traffic Classifiers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07739)
*Zhenpeng Li*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种可控性感知的黑盒迁移攻击框架，用于评估LLM在网络流量分类中的对抗鲁棒性。该框架将流量特征按通信语义分为直接可控、间接可控和不可控三类，仅扰动直接可控特征。利用XGBoost替代模型生成三种对抗样本，迁移攻击7个LLM目标和2个传统ML目标，在5个IDS基准数据集上生成超过50万对抗样本。结果表明，LLM的迁移脆弱性显著但与数据集和比较对象相关，且梯度/score-based扰动比贪婪扰动迁移效果更好，约束违反率为0%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM作为网络入侵检测分类器的潜力正被探索，但其在现实攻击者约束下的对抗鲁棒性尚不明确。

Method: 提出可控性感知的黑盒迁移攻击框架，将流量特征按网络通信语义分为DC、IC、UC三类，仅扰动DC特征。使用共享XGBoost替代模型，生成finite-difference PGD、greedy coordinate-wise和NES三种对抗样本，迁移攻击7个LLM和2个传统ML目标，在5个IDS基准数据集上测试。

Result: LLM的迁移脆弱性显著但受数据集和比较对象影响。相对于LightGBM，LLM在RT-IoT2022和CIC-IDS-2018上更脆弱，在NSL-KDD和UNSW-NB15上相当，在HIKARI-2021上更鲁棒；相对于平均ML基线，LLM在所有5个数据集上ASR更高。梯度/score-based扰动比贪婪扰动迁移效果更好。跨替代模型验证结果一致，约束违反率为0%。

Conclusion: LLM作为网络入侵检测分类器存在显著的对抗迁移脆弱性，但脆弱程度因数据集和比较基准而异；梯度信息驱动的攻击更具威胁性；所提框架可有效生成符合现实约束的对抗样本。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly explored as network intrusion detection classifiers, but their adversarial robustness under realistic attacker constraints remains unclear. We present a controllability-aware black-box transfer framework for LLM-based network traffic classifiers. The framework partitions flow features into directly controllable (DC), indirectly controllable (IC), and uncontrollable (UC) groups according to network communication semantics, then restricts perturbations to DC features while freezing IC/UC features. Using a shared XGBoost surrogate, we generate finite-difference PGD, greedy coordinate-wise, and NES adversarial examples and transfer them to seven LLM targets and two conventional ML targets across five IDS benchmarks from 1999 to 2022. Across 27 valid LLM configurations and over 500,000 adversarial examples, we find that LLM transfer vulnerability is substantial but dataset- and comparator-dependent. Compared with LightGBM, LLMs are more vulnerable on RT-IoT2022 and CIC-IDS-2018, comparable on NSL-KDD and UNSW-NB15, and less vulnerable on HIKARI-2021; compared with the averaged ML baseline, LLMs show higher ASR on all five datasets. We further observe a consistent cross-architecture transfer hierarchy: gradient- and score-based perturbations transfer more effectively than greedy perturbations across all 27 LLM cells and 9/10 ML cells. Cross-surrogate validation with tree, neural, and linear surrogates yields similar LLM ASR, reducing evidence that the findings are XGBoost-specific. Constraint violation rate is 0\% by construction.

</details>


### [130] [Forensic Schema for Psychological Manipulation in Cyber Fraud: LLM-Driven Victim Reports Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07751)
*Zikai Alex Wen,Corrazon Ogot,Juan Li,Yan Bai*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出一种包含心理操纵指标和加密货币证据的法医分类模式（4类35问），通过LLM对10,994份受害者报告进行标注，发现不同诈骗类型具有显著不同的操纵特征，但存在法医细节缺口，建议采用AI辅助的受害者问询来弥补。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有网络犯罪分类模式仅捕获联系元数据和金融交易，忽略了 perpetrators 使用的心理操纵技术。

Method: 提出包含4类35问的法医分类模式，新增11个操纵指标和加密货币证据字段；使用LLM对10,994份受害者报告进行自动标注，并与两名人类标注者验证。

Result: LLM与人类标注者一致性（κ=0.69）与人类之间一致性（κ=0.68）相当；不同诈骗类型具有显著不同的操纵特征（Cramer's V最高0.790）；存在法医细节缺口——操纵技术检测可靠，但受害者叙述在可操作细节上差异大，区块链标识符几乎缺失。

Conclusion: AI辅助的受害者问询（结合模式指导的追问）是缩小法医细节缺口的最直接途径；分层标注策略可作为其他法医文本领域LLM提取的可复用模板。

Abstract: Existing cybercrime classification schemas capture contact metadata and financial transactions but omit the psychological manipulation techniques perpetrators employ. We present a forensic schema (four categories, 35 questions) adding 11 manipulation indicators and cryptocurrency evidence fields to established forensic foundations. Applied to 10,994 victim reports via large language model (LLM)-driven annotation and validated against two human annotators (mean LLM-human $κ= 0.69$, matching inter-annotator $κ= 0.68$), the schema revealed a statistically distinct manipulation profile for each major fraud type (Cramer's $V$ up to $0.790$). A rationale-based evidence audit nonetheless exposed a forensic detail gap: detection of manipulation techniques was reliable, but victim narratives varied widely in the actionable detail supporting each Yes answer, and blockchain-specific identifiers were nearly absent. These findings point to AI-assisted victim intake with schema-informed follow-up questions as the most direct way to close the gap. The tiered annotation strategy also provides a reusable template for LLM-based extraction from other forensic text domains.

</details>


### [131] [ScopeJudge: Cost-Aware Pre-Execution Gating for Offensive Security Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07774)
*Shane Caldwell,Max Harley,Ads Dawson,Michael Kouremetis,Vincent Abruzzo,Will Pearce*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出ScopeJudge基准，用于评估LLM安全代理在执行前通过轻量级裁判模型检测越界工具调用（scope violations）的能力。基准包含4,897个工具调用（7.7%越界），由专业渗透测试人员标注，评估了8种裁判模型在5种上下文策略下的成本-准确率Pareto前沿。发现静态策略因无法感知用户请求而召回率近乎为零，表明基于请求条件的监控是必要的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM代理在 offensive security（进攻性安全）任务中，一个越界的工具调用可能破坏客户边界、影响生产或导致漏洞赏金失效。边界由用户请求声明且需从意图推断，而非固定策略所能枚举。现有方法缺乏对工具调用执行前的越界检测能力。

Method: 提出预执行门控机制：一个便宜、可信的LLM裁判模型在强大可替换代理执行每个工具调用前对其进行审核（接受/拒绝）。构建ScopeJudge基准：从代理轨迹中收集4,897个工具调用（7.7%越界），由专业渗透测试员标注（Fleiss kappa=0.64）。评估8种裁判模型在5种上下文策略（从仅静态策略到完整原始转录）下的表现，绘制成本-准确率Pareto前沿。

Result: 静态策略因无法感知用户请求，裁判召回率接近零，确认scope来自于请求，请求条件监控是必要的。推荐两个操作点：成本敏感配置和高风险部署的召回优先配置。专家一致性参考点F1=0.78。

Conclusion: 发布ScopeJudge数据集，支持实时监控和可扩展的安全代理自主监督。静态策略在scope强制上结构性地不足，必须采用基于请求条件的监控。

Abstract: As LLM agents take on offensive security work, a single out-of-scope tool call can breach a client's engagement boundary, disrupt production, or void a bug-bounty finding. Unlike a fixed safety policy, the boundary that matters is declared in the user's request and must be inferred from intent. That challenge is sharpened by the adversarial nature of offensive security: the same tool call is in or out of scope depending not on the action itself but on the target it touches and the context in which it runs, which no fixed policy can enumerate in advance. We study pre-execution gating: a cheap, trusted LLM judge inspects each call proposed by a strong, swappable agent, and accepts or rejects it before it runs. We introduce ScopeJudge, a benchmark of 4,897 tool calls (7.7% scope violations) from agent trajectories on tasks engineered to tempt agents out of scope and labeled at the call level by professional penetration testers, with substantial inter-grader agreement (Fleiss kappa = 0.64) that sets an expert agreement reference point of F1 = 0.78. We evaluate eight judge models under five transcript strategies, varying how much context the judge sees, from the static policy alone to the full raw transcript, and chart the resulting cost-accuracy Pareto frontier. We find that a static policy is structurally insufficient for scope enforcement: blind to the user's request, judge recall collapses to near zero, confirming that scope lives in the request and that request-conditioned monitoring is necessary. Because a missed violation costs more than a spurious rejection, we report precision, recall, and F1 separately and recommend two operating points: a cost-sensitive configuration and a recall-first one for high-stakes deployments. We release the ScopeJudge dataset to support real-time monitoring and scalable oversight of autonomous security agents.

</details>


### [132] [Open Models, Open Risks: Measuring Unsafe Generation in Text-to-Image Models In the Wild](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07827)
*Peilin Han,Yang Liu,Yilong Yang,Jingchun Zhang,Teng Li,Jianfeng Ma,Zhuo Ma*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文通过大规模实证研究，评估了200余个真实世界T2I模型在越狱攻击下的安全性。研究发现，传统检测器指标会高估实际风险，因此提出了改进的Advanced ASR指标。结果显示，许多下游模型仍保留一定安全性，但也识别出高风险模型（包括明确NSFW导向和看似良性但存在隐患的模型），并将相关案例报告给了Hugging Face。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有T2I越狱安全研究多限于受控实验室环境和小规模规范模型，面对快速发展的真实世界T2I生态系统，其实际安全状况尚不明确。这种不确定性因检测器指标设计缺陷（不适用于真实场景）和真实风险来源多样化（包括对抗性提示、不安全发布实践和模型衍生）而被放大。

Method: 首先指出现有检测器越狱指标因语义漂移和生成伪影而高估风险，提出Advanced ASR指标以更准确捕捉语义有效且视觉合理的非安全生成。随后使用该改进指标，在三种代表性越狱攻击下系统评估了Hugging Face上200余个真实世界T2I模型。

Result: 许多下游模型即使没有显式的后期安全防护，仍保留非平凡程度的安全性，表明真实世界中安全退化既非普遍也非均匀。同时识别出高风险模型，包括明确NSFW导向的发布和看似良性但通过系统评估才暴露不安全行为的模型。

Conclusion: 真实世界T2I模型的安全性状况复杂：部分模型保持良好安全水平，但存在高风险模型。研究进一步追溯了这些模型的上线背景，并向Hugging Face报告了高风险案例，为生态系统安全治理提供实证基础。

Abstract: Existing safety studies on text-to-image (T2I) jailbreaks are largely conducted in controlled in-the-lab settings, typically on a small number of canonical models. As a result, the current safety status of the rapidly growing in-the-wild T2I ecosystem remains unclear. This uncertainty is amplified by two factors: existing detector-based metrics are designed for controlled evaluation, and in-the-wild risks may arise not only from adversarial prompting, but also from unsafe release practices and unsafe model derivatives.
  In this paper, we present a large-scale empirical study of in-the-wild T2I safety through the lens of jailbreak. We first show that detector-only jailbreak metrics substantially overestimate practical risk over in the wild due to semantic drift and generation artifacts, and we introduce Advanced ASR to better capture semantically valid and visually plausible unsafe generation. Using this refined metric, we evaluate 200+ in-the-wild T2I models from Hugging Face under three representative jailbreak attacks. Our results show that many downstream models retain a non-trivial degree of safety even without explicit post-hoc safeguards, indicating that safety degradation in the wild is neither universal nor uniform. At the same time, we identify a set of high-risk models, including explicitly NSFW-oriented releases as well as seemingly benign models whose unsafe behavior is only exposed through systematic evaluation. We further trace these models to their release context and report high-risk cases to Hugging Face.

</details>


### [133] [Closed-Loop Dynamic Validator Node Scaling in Private Substrate Blockchains Using Takagi-Sugeno Fuzzy Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07901)
*Thandile Nododile,Ayinde M. Usman,Clement N. Nyirenda*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于Takagi-Sugeno模糊推理系统的私有区块链验证者数量动态调节方法，通过读取实时区块链参数（区块生成时间、区块大小、活跃节点数），输出效率评分和扩缩容建议。在10节点Substrate网络上用真实水务数据验证，相比阈值法具有更稳定的扩缩容行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 私有区块链网络采用固定节点配置，无法适应动态变化的工作负载：节点过多浪费资源，节点过少导致区块生产变慢和最终确认延迟，且最优验证者数量难以确定。

Method: 采用Takagi-Sugeno模糊推理系统，输入三个实时区块链参数（区块生产时间、区块大小、活跃节点数），使用三角形隶属函数和完整的27条规则库（乘积t-范数聚合），输出连续效率评分和扩缩容建议。关键贡献是经验性地重新校准隶属函数，将其锚定到测试床的实际运行范围而非理论极值。

Result: 在4、7、10个活跃节点的配置上统计验证了控制器能产生区分性的操作剖面；闭环实验中控制器能自主双向调节验证者参与度，从两个方向收敛到相同稳定均衡；相比三个阈值基线方法，扩缩容振荡更少，同时保持可比的区块生产时间。

Conclusion: TS模糊推理能够支持私有区块链部署中的自主验证者管理，具有阈值方法无法实现的稳定扩缩容行为。

Abstract: Private blockchain networks run with fixed node configurations that cannot adapt to changing workload conditions. Too many nodes serving a light workload waste resources; too few nodes facing heavy demand slow block production and degrade finalisation. The right validator count is hard to determine, as it depends on overlapping factors that shift over time. This paper presents a Takagi-Sugeno (TS) fuzzy inference system that reads live blockchain parameters (block production time, block size, and active node count) and outputs a continuous efficiency score alongside a scaling recommendation: Scale Up, Maintain, or Scale Down. The controller uses triangular membership functions across three linguistic variables, evaluated through a complete 27-rule base with product t-norm aggregation. A key contribution is an empirical recalibration of the membership functions, anchoring linguistic terms to the observed operating range of the testbed rather than to theoretical extremes. The system is evaluated on a 10-node Substrate blockchain network storing real smart water meter data hashes from the Queensland Government open data portal. Statistical analysis across configurations of 4, 7, and 10 active nodes confirms that the controller produces distinct operational profiles reflecting each configuration's provisioning state. In closed-loop experiments, the controller autonomously adjusts validator participation in both directions, activating validators under rising load and removing them under over-provisioning, converging to the same stable equilibrium from both directions. Compared against three threshold-based baselines, it shows fewer scaling oscillations while maintaining comparable block production times. Results show that TS fuzzy inference can support autonomous validator management in private blockchain deployments, with stable scaling behaviour threshold approaches cannot match.

</details>


### [134] [Mechanistic Interpretability of LLM Jailbreaks via Internal Attribution Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07903)
*Anupam Wagle,Ifrat Ikhtear Uddin,Chaowei Zhang,Longwei Wang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于内部计算图的机制性框架，用于诊断大语言模型对对抗性提示和越狱攻击的脆弱性。通过构建和对比干净与攻击提示下的计算图，揭示攻击如何抑制安全相关组件、引入攻击特定特征并重路由计算路径，并基于此进行因果干预以提升模型鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法主要通过输入-输出行为或归因方法分析LLM的对抗性失败，无法深入理解对抗扰动如何改变模型内部推理机制，导致不安全或错误行为背后的机理仍不清楚。

Method: 提出一个机制性框架：(1) 将计算分解为不变、抑制和涌现结构；(2) 识别与失败模式相关的反复出现的脆弱性主题；(3) 对节点、路径和子图进行因果干预，直接评估其对攻击成功的贡献。核心是构建和配准干净与攻击提示下的内部计算图。

Result: 在多个开源LLM和多种对抗/越狱基准上的实验表明，内部计算图的结构偏差与不安全行为高度相关。对识别出的脆弱性主题进行靶向干预可有效提升模型鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 内部计算图为理解、诊断和缓解LLM脆弱性提供了原则性基础，实现了从描述性归因到因果诊断的转变。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) exhibit remarkable capabilities but remain highly vulnerable to adversarial prompts and jailbreak attacks. Existing approaches primarily analyze these failures through input-output behaviors or attribution methods, offering limited insight into how adversarial perturbations alter the model's internal reasoning. Consequently, the mechanisms underlying unsafe or incorrect behaviors remain poorly understood. We introduce a mechanistic framework for diagnosing LLM vulnerabilities using paired internal computation graphs, which represent prompt-specific inference as structured causal interactions among latent features. By constructing and aligning computation graphs for clean and attacked prompts, we reveal that adversarial attacks induce systematic transformations of internal reasoning, including suppression of safety-relevant components, emergence of attack-specific features, and rerouting of computation paths. Building on this representation, we propose a unified framework that (i) decomposes computation into invariant, suppressed, and emergent structures, (ii) identifies recurring vulnerability motifs associated with failure modes, and (iii) performs causal interventions on nodes, paths, and subgraphs to directly evaluate their contributions to attack success. This enables a transition from descriptive attribution to causal diagnosis of model failures.
